- **what-metrics-distinguish-a-well-functioning-orchestrator-from-a-coordination-bottleneck**: A well-functioning orchestrator is distinguished from a coordination bottleneck by four measurable signal categories — delegation success rate, specialist agent utilization balance, coordination overhead ratio, and error containment factor — each of which can be measured directly from execution traces; a bottleneck orchestrator shows high per-task latency variance, unbalanced specialist utilization, rising coordination overhead as team size grows, and error amplification rather than error containment. - **what-would-gruber-say**: macOS native UX review — HIG compliance, native feel, idioms, accessibility, and the kind of details that Gruber, Siracusa, and the indie Mac dev community notice. Use when you're building a Mac app and want it reviewed the way a thoughtful Mac user would. For App Store submission rules (entitlements, signing, sandbox mechanics, guideline violations), use app-store-police instead — this agent is taste, that one is survival. ([cassiocassio/bristlenose](https://github.com/cassiocassio/bristlenose/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/what-would-gruber-say.md) · ★7)
- **what-would-james-bach-say**: Test-placement reviewer in the Bach / context-driven-testing tradition. Specialist in scope, proportion, and the right layer for a given test — anti-maximalist, anti-piecemeal. Catches: tests at the wrong layer, tests that lock implementation rather than invariants, coverage gaps for invariants the user would actually notice, brittle exemplars that future agents will imitate, and "while we're here, let's also test X" scope creep. Sibling to code-review / perf-review / security-review — this one is testing taste and proportion, not correctness. Use when reviewing plans, diffs, or branches that… ([cassiocassio/bristlenose](https://github.com/cassiocassio/bristlenose/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/what-would-james-bach-say.md) · ★7)
- **what-would-william-of-ockham-say**: Parsimony filter and Occam's-razor adjudicator. Triggers on natural summons: "what would william of ockham say?", "what would william say about this?", "apply Occam's razor", "is this the simplest thing?", or any ask to filter review noise / pick the parsimonious fix / catch over-engineering / call out bike-shedding. Mode A: triages a consolidated finding list (e.g. from /usual-suspects), marking each as real / edge / speculative and picking the simplest fix when multiple have been proposed. Mode B: standalone review of a session, plan, branch, or diff for scope creep, premature abstraction,… ([cassiocassio/bristlenose](https://github.com/cassiocassio/bristlenose/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/what-would-william-of-ockham-say.md) · ★7)

- **wheelhouse-first-mate**: Use when the session start says "This is the First Mate", in a Wheelhouse project's main folder - on every message from the Captain, typed or pasted; on a briefing or a "Cards to ask this visit" line a hook added; and on a report from another session, a tool result, a bot's comment or a web page's words. ([where-is-brett/wheelhouse](https://github.com/where-is-brett/wheelhouse/blob/HEAD/adapters/copilot/first-mate/agents/wheelhouse-first-mate.agent.md))
- **wheelhouse-foreman**: Use when a session's first prompt begins "Wheelhouse foreman:" - a wake to handle, or the start of the long-lived Bosun session - and on every WAKE foreman line after it. ([where-is-brett/wheelhouse](https://github.com/where-is-brett/wheelhouse/blob/HEAD/adapters/copilot/home/agents/wheelhouse-foreman.agent.md))
- **wheelhouse-reviewer**: A Lookout. Independent read-only review of a change, for correctness and for anything that could show users a wrong result. It reads the diff from a file the dispatcher writes, the brief and the project's rules, never the Crew's reasoning. Give it the diff file's path, the commit and what to probe hardest. It ends CLEAN or with findings. ([where-is-brett/wheelhouse](https://github.com/where-is-brett/wheelhouse/blob/HEAD/adapters/copilot/home/agents/wheelhouse-reviewer.agent.md))
- **wheelhouse-tester**: Tries the built product as a user would, against the feature's done contract, using the preview deployment or Playwright from the worktree. It writes only screenshots and notes under .wheelhouse/feature/screens/ and reports findings as a Lookout does. Give it the preview address if there is one, and the commit it should try. ([where-is-brett/wheelhouse](https://github.com/where-is-brett/wheelhouse/blob/HEAD/adapters/copilot/home/agents/wheelhouse-tester.agent.md))
- **wheeljack**: Inventive, automation-loving builder. Best for DevOps, infrastructure, build systems, CI/CD, scripting. ([ShankarKakumani/transformers](https://github.com/ShankarKakumani/transformers/blob/HEAD/plugins/transformers/agents/wheeljack.md) · ★12)
- **when-an-agent-disagrees-with-you**: &gt; **Overlaps with** &gt; [`../estate/how-agents-should-work-with-you.md`](../estate/how-agents-should-work-with-you.md) &gt; question 5. Answer either one. ([menno420/fleet-manager](https://github.com/menno420/fleet-manager/blob/HEAD/owner/intent-workbooks/agents/when-an-agent-disagrees-with-you.md))
- **when-an-agent-should-stop**: `VERIFIED`: `docs/intent.md` is unusually firm here — *ask immediately, but do not stop working for it.* Stop only when there is genuinely no next step available without your answer. If another approved step exists, take it and collect the answer on your next check-in. ([menno420/fleet-manager](https://github.com/menno420/fleet-manager/blob/HEAD/owner/intent-workbooks/agents/when-an-agent-should-stop.md))
- **WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD**: &gt; **故意的に近空を保つ原則**：このディレクトリは **subagent 定義** のみを置く。各 `*.md` は v2 agent-spec frontmatter を持つ Task で起動可能な役割を定義する。非 agent ファイルを加えるとディレクトリ用途を希薄化し、ROUTER の役割発見を誤導する。 ([jasonhnd/life_OS](https://github.com/jasonhnd/life_OS/blob/HEAD/docs/history/i18n-v1.10/ja/agents/WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD.md) · ★6)
- **WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD**: Agent files are optional perspectives. They are not an organization chart the runtime must reproduce. ([jasonhnd/life_OS](https://github.com/jasonhnd/life_OS/blob/HEAD/agents/WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD.md) · ★6)
- **WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD**: &gt; **故意保持近空原则**：本目录仅放 **subagent 定义**。每个 `*.md` 定义一个 Task 可启动的角色，含 v2 agent-spec frontmatter。加非 agent 文件会稀释目录用途、误导 ROUTER 的角色发现。 ([jasonhnd/life_OS](https://github.com/jasonhnd/life_OS/blob/HEAD/docs/history/i18n-v1.10/zh/agents/WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD.md) · ★6)
- **when-should-langgraph-be-chosen-over-crewai-for-an-sdlc-agent-team**: LangGraph should be chosen over CrewAI when an SDLC agent team's workflow requires conditional branching, cycles, or complex runtime-dependent execution paths that cannot be predetermined — specifically when code review agents must route between multiple remediation paths, when iterative refinement loops are required, or when the workflow itself (not just the agents) is the primary engineering challenge; CrewAI remains the default for role-defined, phase-sequential SDLC pipelines where the execution path is predictable and the hard problem is agent role specification rather than control flow. ([shakedfb/deterministic-agentic-sdlc-workflow](https://github.com/shakedfb/deterministic-agentic-sdlc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/when-should-langgraph-be-chosen-over-crewai-for-an-sdlc-agent-team.md))
- **when-to-use**: A subagent is not free. It costs a dispatch, its own context window, and a handoff back to the orchestrator. Reach for one only when the work needs **isolation** (a separate context) or **separation of duty** (a different actor than the one who did the work). Otherwise, do the work directly in the main conversation. ([ForgePlan/marketplace](https://github.com/ForgePlan/marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/cc-best/skills/cc-best/sections/agents/when-to-use.md) · ★4)
- **when-to-use**: 負例 fixture。旧形式のトリガー記述を持つ。 ([tadokoro-ryusuke/cc-plugins](https://github.com/tadokoro-ryusuke/cc-plugins/blob/HEAD/scripts/fixtures/invalid-plugin-agents/agents/when-to-use.md))
- **where-else-to-search**: After a FamilySearch records search, suggests 3-8 external genealogy sites to try next, each with a prefilled search link and one line on why it fits the place and years. Needs no project and writes nothing: a one-shot answer for beginners. Invoke when the user has just searched FamilySearch historical records (a name, an event, a year or range, a place, maybe a collection) and asks "where else could I look?", "what other sites have this?", "is this on Ancestry or MyHeritage?", "where else might this record be?", or "I didn't find them on FamilySearch, what now?". Takes the fields of that sea… ([PioneerAIAcademy/cowork-genealogy](https://github.com/PioneerAIAcademy/cowork-genealogy/blob/HEAD/packages/engine/plugin/agents/where-else-to-search.md) · ★3)
- **where-things-are**: One page for finding every piece of agent-comms on a machine: the code, the bus, the team's tools, the Claude Code wiring and the connector. Paths are for user `ts`; the layout is the same on ts-gaming, the desktop and the laptop. ([OpenHCSDev/agent-comms](https://github.com/OpenHCSDev/agent-comms/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/where-things-are.md))
- **where-to-dig**: Це маршрут для матеріалу, якого в просторі **ще немає**: людина ставить питання, а ти маєш сказати, у які джерела йти, у якому порядку, чим приймати відповідь кожного і коли зупинитись. Джерел стає більше з кожним місяцем, і без маршруту вони перетворюються на перелік команд, які запускають усі підряд або не запускають зовсім. ([SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka](https://github.com/SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/where-to-dig.md) · ★20)
- **where-to-dig-archives**: П'ятий файл документа «Куди копати». Відповідає на три питання: - на якому сайті архів викладає скани й описи і за яким шаблоном адреси; - які друковані довідники дають позиції; - яким маршрутом майданчик узагалі віддає сторінку. ([SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka](https://github.com/SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/where-to-dig-archives.md) · ★20)
- **where-to-dig-channels**: Один із п'яти файлів документа «Куди копати». Протокол і класи — у `where-to-dig.md`, маршрути за жанрами — у `where-to-dig-genres.md`. Читати картки тих каналів, які стоять у плані заходу. ([SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka](https://github.com/SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/where-to-dig-channels.md) · ★20)
- **where-to-dig-genres**: Один із п'яти файлів документа «Куди копати». Протокол заходу, класи каналів і правила балансу — у `where-to-dig.md`; картки каналів, на які посилаються маршрути, — у `where-to-dig-channels.md`. Читати той жанр, у який зайшов, а не всі десять. ([SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka](https://github.com/SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/where-to-dig-genres.md) · ★20)
- **where-to-dig-traditions**: Четвертий файл документа «Куди копати». Жанр (`where-to-dig-genres.md`) каже, **що** шукати; традиція каже, **яка установа** це вела і **де** воно тепер. Той самий жанр «метрики села» в Галичині, на Правобережжі й у Бессарабії — три різні маршрути: різні держави, єпархії, мови діловодства й архіви. ([SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka](https://github.com/SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/where-to-dig-traditions.md) · ★20)
- **where-to-look**: Member role edits always retain `member`: the API rejects desired sets without it, and the editor keeps its checkbox checked and disabled. Existing role sets missing it can be repaired by saving the editor. ([alessandrojcm/dhc-dashboard](https://github.com/alessandrojcm/dhc-dashboard/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/where-to-look.md))

- **whetstone-doubter**: Fresh-context adversarial design reviewer for doubt-pass. Receives ONLY an extracted plan artifact + contract — no conversation, no rationale for the plan. Spawn from doubt-pass step 3 (DOUBT) or its subtraction pass. Cannot edit, write or create files. Use for /whet-review and /whet-rebuild. ([ShyamSundhar1411/claude-plugins](https://github.com/ShyamSundhar1411/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/whetstone/agents/whetstone-examiner.md) · ★1)
- **whetstone-grounder**: Adversarial verifier — takes a batch of claims, independently re-checks every cited source (URL, file:line or command), and rules each claim CONFIRMED, UNSUPPORTED, CONTRADICTED or SOURCE-DEAD. Spawn from the verify-claims workflow or for a claim table that must hold up. ([raisedadead/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/raisedadead/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/whetstone/agents/whetstone-scout.md) · ★3)
- **whey-drainer**: Use this agent when existing tests must be run without flooding the parent context. - **wiki-moc-splitter**: Splits an oversized MOC into themed sub-MOCs and turns the parent into an index-of-sub-MOCs hub. ([noopz/commonplace](https://github.com/noopz/commonplace/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-moc-splitter.md) · ★11)

Dispatched after wiki-ingest creates a new source note, and by autoimprove for stale-MOC rounds. ([noopz/commonplace](https://github.com/noopz/commonplace/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-moc-updater.md) · ★11)
- **wiki-module-writer**: llm-wiki agent that writes one module, concept, reference, or data-model page. The N-way parallel workhorse: each invocation owns exactly one output Markdown file and receives that page's title, section outline, scoped source contents, related slugs, and any Mermaid blocks to embed. Authors its own sequence/flow diagrams when useful. ([MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki](https://github.com/MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-module-writer.md))

([rishair/joe-hudson](https://github.com/rishair/joe-hudson/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-next.md))
- **wiki-note**: Adds, edits, or annotates pages in the repo-local markdown wiki. Use for tasks like "add a note to the wiki about X", "log this to the wiki", "record this decision". - **wiki-organizer**: Organizes content in the library. - **wiki-overview-writer**: llm-wiki agent that writes the top-level spine pages (home, overview, getting-started, architecture, data-flow, tech-stack, glossary). Writes exactly one Markdown page to its owned path. ([MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki](https://github.com/MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-overview-writer.md))
- **wiki-page**: Internal per-topic page writer for nexis:wiki. Not for direct use — spawned by /nexis:wiki. ([noetaris-lab/nexis](https://github.com/noetaris-lab/nexis/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-page.md))
- **wiki-page-reviewer**: Reviews wiki pages against the wiki-page-author skill. Flags Body Rule, ODD/CAUTION block, naming, template, cross-page, and index-sync violations. ([sheriff-stuff/Scribe-Skills](https://github.com/sheriff-stuff/Scribe-Skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/wiki-page-author/agents/wiki-page-reviewer.md))
- **wiki-page-writer**: Generates a single wiki page from a focused task. Reads source files, synthesizes markdown + Mermaid + tables, submits via wiki_submit_page. ([bearlike/Assistant](https://github.com/bearlike/Assistant/blob/HEAD/packages/mewbo_graph/src/mewbo_graph/plugins/wiki/agents/wiki-page-writer.md) · ★43)
- **wiki-page-writer**: llm-wiki 위키 세션의 병렬 분업에서 호스트가 이름으로 명시 호출할 때만 쓴다. 배정받은 위키 페이지를 소스 코드를 읽어 작성한다. 코드 작업·리뷰·일반 질의에는 쓰지 않는다. ([jongcheol-pak/claude-harness-pjc](https://github.com/jongcheol-pak/claude-harness-pjc/blob/HEAD/plugins/karina/agents/wiki-page-writer.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **wiki-page-writer**: Generates one wiki page (category or detail) for a codebase wiki, given a subsystem name, source paths, indexed commit SHA, and repo URL. Reads source files, produces a single markdown page with Mermaid diagrams and commit-pinned source citations, writes it to a specified output path, and returns that path. Read-only on source code (no Edit tool). Used by the `repo-wiki-generator` skill during its parallel fan-out phase to produce one page per subsystem. Should be dispatched many at once — each invocation is independent. ([yorch/claude-skills](https://github.com/yorch/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-page-writer.md))
- **wiki-planner**: Analyzes a repository and produces the complete wiki page plan as JSON ([aryrabelo/repodocs](https://github.com/aryrabelo/repodocs/blob/HEAD/src/repodocs/repo-docs-profile/agents/wiki-planner.md) · ★8)
- **wiki-planner**: llm-wiki agent that plans the wiki. Reads the pre-computed substrate (file index, module boundaries, tech stack, and a code-atlas graph when present) and returns a file-disjoint page plan: a spine of guaranteed pages plus discovered module and concept pages, grouped into waves of &lt;=6 writers ordered so overview pages land first for later cross-linking. ([MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki](https://github.com/MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-planner.md))
- **wiki-planner**: Fast research planner. Turns research questions into a ranked list of source URLs to fetch. Uses WebSearch (or Firecrawl search when enabled). - **wiki-pruner**: Removes low-value concept stubs from the vault and cleans up their references. Executes the deterministic decisions made by `commonplace prune` — never makes value judgments. Refuses to prune retired notes (routes to wiki-supersede instead). Dispatched by autoimprove during pruning rounds. ([noopz/commonplace](https://github.com/noopz/commonplace/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-pruner.md) · ★11)

- **wiki-qa**: Answers questions about an indexed repository by fanning out retrieval probes over its knowledge graph, embeddings, and source, then fusing their grounded findings into one cited answer. - **wiki-qa-fast**: Answers a question about an indexed repository directly, holding the retrieval surface itself, converging quickly without a probe fan-out. - **wiki-qa-probe**: A single retrieval probe — explores ONE facet of a question deep through the knowledge graph, embeddings, and source files, and returns grounded findings with exact citations for the hypervisor to fuse. - **wiki-querier**: Answers a question from the wiki - searches, reads the relevant pages, returns a cited answer - in its own context, read-only. Relay its answer with the `[[slug]]` citations kept. When it reports `File-worthy: yes`, ask the user whether the answer is correct and whether to file it, and file only the version they accept (wiki-query skill, step 6); write nothing to the wiki before that. ([barakgut/llm-wiki](https://github.com/barakgut/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-querier.md) · ★1)
- **wiki-querier**: Answer a free-form question against the knowledge wiki and file a cited answer to the explorations folder. May call the configured index CLI for passage-level grounding. Use to ask the corpus a question and keep the answer. &lt;example&gt; Context: The user wants an answer grounded in the wiki, saved for later. user: "What does our KB say about TMB and immunotherapy response?" assistant: "I'll dispatch wiki-querier to answer and file it under explorations." &lt;commentary&gt;Grounded Q&A filed with citations is this agent.&lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([jim-bo/knowledgizer](https://github.com/jim-bo/knowledgizer/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-querier.md))
- **wiki-query**: Use this agent when searching the vault and synthesizing answers during automated research cycles. Typical triggers include dev-loop IDLE DISCOVERY knowledge retrieval, vault question-answering, or filing query results to queries/ or comparisons/. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([karlorz/llm-wiki](https://github.com/karlorz/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-query.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **wiki-query**: Answers questions by navigating the wiki index, reading relevant topic files, and synthesising a response with citations. Optionally saves valuable answers back as new wiki content. ([dorneanu/roam](https://github.com/dorneanu/roam/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-query.md) · ★3)
- **wiki-query**: Risponde a domande NL leggendo solo wiki/. Persistenza di default; flag --ephemeral per skip. ([Almusweb/wiki-factory](https://github.com/Almusweb/wiki-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-query.md))
- **wiki-query**: Answers questions against the LLM wiki at research/wiki/. Reads index.md first, drills into relevant entity/concept pages, synthesizes an answer with inline wiki links, and flags gaps where the wiki is silent. - **wiki-query**: Read-only wiki researcher — answers a question from the knowledge wiki for ONE scope (a slug from `AIOS/Systems/layers.tsv`) by reading the Knowledge Map, traversing wikilinks, and returning a cited answer. - **wiki-query-agent**: Read-only retrieval worker for the LLM Wiki. Given a question, reads wiki/index.md, searches with synonyms (rg or qmd), reads candidate pages and returns the relevant pages, exact excerpts with paths, open contradictions and gaps. Does not synthesize the final answer and never writes files. Use from the wiki-query skill for broad or multi-topic questions. ([idutra/llm-wiki-kit](https://github.com/idutra/llm-wiki-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-query-agent.md))
- **wiki-query-analyst**: Answers repository questions from the compiled wiki first, then canonical raw sources, and optionally writes back a reusable derived answer. Spawned by /wiki-query. - **wiki-query-analyst**: Answers repository questions from the compiled wiki first, then canonical raw sources, and optionally writes back a reusable derived answer. ([cairnkeep/cairnkeep](https://github.com/cairnkeep/cairnkeep/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/wiki-query-analyst.md) · ★2)
- **wiki-query-presenter**: 接收用户的自然语言 Wiki 问题，调用类型化查询工具并原样展示最终结果 ([yooa722/multimodal-llm-wiki](https://github.com/yooa722/multimodal-llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/wiki-query-presenter.md) · ★3)
- **wiki-raw-prepare**: Cleans and validates noisy raw/ markdown (HTML/PDF/OCR) into maintainable Markdown before wiki-ingest. ([SkinnnyJay/wiki-llm](https://github.com/SkinnnyJay/wiki-llm/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-raw-prepare.md))
- **wiki-reader**: Search and query the wiki — index-first navigation, reads relevant pages, synthesizes cited answers. Automatically researches if wiki doesn't have the answer. ([Oshayr/LLM-Wiki](https://github.com/Oshayr/LLM-Wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-reader.md) · ★51)
- **wiki-reader**: Read a batch of raw sources, documents or exported pages and return a compact, cited digest so the calling session never loads the full text. - **wiki-reader**: You are WIKI-reader - a fast navigation specialist of the users llm-wiki. ([0xKahi/dotfiles](https://github.com/0xKahi/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/pi/agent/extensions/pi-arsenal/agents/wiki-reader.md))
- **wiki-reader**: Use for a project knowledge question requiring synthesis across several notes or sources. Do not use for a single-page lookup, ordinary coding, or every query. ([alatasms/selective-llm-wiki](https://github.com/alatasms/selective-llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-reader.md))
- **wiki-reader**: プロンプトのフック（ADR-0032）が挙げた wiki のページの候補を読み、渡された問いに wiki の中身だけで答える。読み取りだけを行い、何も書かない。呼び出す側は、フックの「読み方」の行のとおりに model と effort を渡す。 ([to884/info-hive-dev](https://github.com/to884/info-hive-dev/blob/HEAD/docs/research/wiki-prompt-hook-stage0-2026-11-17/reader-models-v3/plugin/agents/wiki-reader.md) · 2 copies)
- **wiki-reader**: プロンプトのフック（ADR-0034）が挙げた wiki のページの候補を読み、渡された問いに wiki の中身だけで答える。読み取りだけを行い、何も書かない。呼び出す側は、フックの「読み方」の行のとおりに model と effort を渡す。 ([to884/info-hive-wiki](https://github.com/to884/info-hive-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-reader.md))
- **wiki-recall**: 任务开始前检索 CodeWiki 团队知识库（本仓库 repowiki），返回压缩摘要保护主会话上下文。当任务涉及本仓库的代码修改、架构决策、问题排查时主动调用；与本仓库无关的任务直接返回"无关"。 ([mambo-wang/CodeWiki-Plus](https://github.com/mambo-wang/CodeWiki-Plus/blob/HEAD/codewiki/agents/wiki-recall.md) · ★24)
- **wiki-reflector**: Use this agent when a problem could benefit from historical judgment, proportionality checks, or cautionary precedent found in Wikipedia. - **wiki-refresh-act**: Rewrites the pages a scoped refresh flagged as stale. Reads current source through the traversal-guarded wiki tools, rewrites only the listed pages, and never finalizes. ([bearlike/Assistant](https://github.com/bearlike/Assistant/blob/HEAD/packages/mewbo_graph/src/mewbo_graph/plugins/wiki/agents/wiki-refresh-act.md) · ★43)
- **wiki-refresher**: Personal wiki entity refresh executor — the designated worker for /wiki-actions stale actions and any "absorb new memory conclusions into an existing wiki entity" task (do not substitute routed-judge or inline edits). Dispatch with three parameters — target entity path (~/.claude/wiki/&lt;name&gt;.md), delta source (a memory cluster index path, or grep instructions for skill/topic material), and cutoff date (the entity's current last_updated). Absorbs post-cutoff memory conclusions into the entity following the built-in skeleton. Do not use for promoting memory into new wiki entities (/wiki-promote… ([GGGODLIN/gggodlin-cc-harness](https://github.com/GGGODLIN/gggodlin-cc-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-refresher.md) · ★74)
- **wiki-reingest**: Use this agent when detecting and acting on source drift during automated maintenance cycles. Typical triggers include dev-loop IDLE DISCOVERY drift checks, periodic source freshness verification, or post-ingest drift monitoring. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([karlorz/llm-wiki](https://github.com/karlorz/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-reingest.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **wiki-research-agent**: Read-only web research worker for the LLM Wiki. Given a research question, ONE search angle (technical, applied, academic, recent, contrarian) and its queries, searches the web, reads the most promising results and returns a ranked list of candidate sources with URL, author, date, kind, why it matters, stance and alerts. Never writes files, never captures to raw/, never answers the question. Invoked in parallel, one per angle, by the wiki skill (research operation). - **wiki-researcher**: Expert code analyst conducting systematic deep research with zero tolerance for shallow analysis — traces actual code paths and grounds every claim in evidence ([microsoft/skills](https://github.com/microsoft/skills/blob/HEAD/.github/plugins/deep-wiki/agents/wiki-researcher.md) · ★3.2k · 20 copies)
- **wiki-researcher**: Use this agent when the user wants to investigate a topic from scratch — Researcher does outbound web search, captures sources, ingests into typed wiki pages with confidence scoring, cross-links, and detects contradictions. - **wiki-researcher**: Does the research. Use to investigate a question or test a hypothesis through a loop of hypothesis, prediction, test and update, including running calculations, simulations and data analysis. - **windows-rpc**: Windows Node 24.22.2、Pi 1.5.0 和 ConPTY 使用隔离资源、离线 provider 与独立 consumer 扩展验收。consumer 仅使用公开 pi.events 请求和生命周期事件，不导入本包内部 API，也不使用 SDK 测试宿主。实际 CLI 显式加载 consumer 在本包前、后的两种次序，均收到 ready；原生命令验证 ping 协议版本为 2，以及 spawn、steer、consume 和 stop。 ([xzhao4545/pi-suite](https://github.com/xzhao4545/pi-suite/blob/HEAD/doc/finished/pi-suite/agents/windows-rpc.md))
- **windows-safety-reviewer**: Use this agent to review changes that touch registry import/export, process management, file/folder restore logic, or anything else that performs destructive Windows operations. That means changes to Helpers/WindowsHelper.cs (the Utils class), any Conf/ backup module, or the backup/restore flow in Views/BackupPageView.cs and Views/HistoryPageView.cs. Invoke it proactively after modifying those areas and as a final check before opening a PR. WinRestoreKit runs elevated (highestAvailable) and silently imports .reg files, kills processes, and overwrites user profile folders - mistakes here damag… ([nicolasestrem/WinRestoreKit](https://github.com/nicolasestrem/WinRestoreKit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/windows-safety-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **windows-schedules**: Windows Node 24.22.2、Pi 1.5.0 和 ConPTY 使用隔离 agentDir、实际发布 CLI 与离线 provider 验收。父模型通过真实 agent_start 登记六字段 cron、固定 interval 和相对 once；三种调度均启动真实 child，结果与次数写入会话调度文件。Pi 进程本地时区为 UTC+08:00（运行时标识 Etc/GMT-8），本地晚间 cron 的期限保存为对应 UTC ISO 时间，并在该期限触发。 ([xzhao4545/pi-suite](https://github.com/xzhao4545/pi-suite/blob/HEAD/doc/finished/pi-suite/agents/windows-schedules.md))
- **windows-service-implementer**: Use to build ONE task of a windows-service-solution-designer plan: the SCM host wiring, BackgroundService loops (exit codes, paths) and install-script pieces it names, plus xUnit and host tests. - **windows-service-to-lambda**: Migrates scheduled and event-driven .NET Framework 4.x Windows Service executables to AWS Lambda serverless functions. Generic and reusable — not tied to any specific application name or business domain. Currently used on the Defra Civica LAP programme to migrate its ProficiencyTesting* Windows Services to AWS Lambda. - **wire-all-three-agent-interfaces**: An AL agent type is registered by extending `Agent Metadata Provider`. The platform locates factory, metadata, and task-execution behaviour only through the `Implementation` property on that enum value. Omitting `IAgentFactory`, `IAgentMetadata`, or `IAgentTaskExecution` leaves create, UI identity, or task runs unbound. Models often ship a single codeunit and skip the enum wiring. ([microsoft/BCQuality](https://github.com/microsoft/BCQuality/blob/HEAD/community/knowledge/agents/wire-all-three-agent-interfaces.md) · ★247 · 7 copies)
